  hw.module @TRIBUF(in %in : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>, in %enable : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>, out out : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>) {

      
    %0 = llhd.constant_time <0ns, 0d, 1e>

      
    %true = hw.constant true

      
    %1 = hw.aggregate_constant [true, true] : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %in_0 = llhd.sig name "in" %1 : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %2 = hw.struct_create (%value_1, %unknown_2) : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    llhd.drv %in_0, %2 after %0 : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %3 = llhd.sig.struct_extract %in_0["value"] : <!hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>>

      
    %4 = llhd.sig.struct_extract %in_0["unknown"] : <!hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>>

      
    %value = hw.struct_extract %enable["value"] : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %unknown = hw.struct_extract %enable["unknown"] : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %5 = comb.xor %unknown, %true : i1

      
    %6 = comb.and %value, %5 : i1

      
    %7 = llhd.prb %3 : i1

      
    %8 = llhd.prb %4 : i1

      
    %9 = comb.xor %6, %true : i1

      
    %10 = comb.or %9, %7 : i1

      
    %11 = comb.and %5, %10 : i1

      
    %12 = comb.or %unknown, %9, %8 : i1

      
    %13 = hw.struct_create (%11, %12) : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %value_1 = hw.struct_extract %in["value"] : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    %unknown_2 = hw.struct_extract %in["unknown"] : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>

      
    hw.output %13 : !hw.struct<value: i1, unknown: i1>
